{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01WJg19RXUtC1M5KaLW2pYeA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have everything I need. Let me rewrite the eval script.\n\nThe input to the preprocessor is a flat dict with keys:\n- `observation.images.image`: base camera [C, H, W] float32 [0,1]\n- `observation.images.image2`: wrist camera [C, H, W] float32 [0,1]\n- `observation.state`: robot state tensor [8]\n- `task`: task description string (this goes into complementary_data)\n\nThe preprocessor pipeline does:\n1. RenameObservations (no-op)\n\n\n2. AddBatchDimension - adds [1, ...] batch dim\n3. Normalizer - normalizes state with mean/std (images are identity)\n4. Pi05PrepareStateTokenizer - discretizes state, creates prompt string\n5. Tokenizer - tokenizes with PaliGemma tokenizer\n6. Device - moves to cuda\n\nThe output batch has:\n- `observation.images.image`: [1, C, H, W] float32 [0,1]\n- `observation.images.image2`: [1, C, H, W] float32 [0,1]\n- `observation.state`: [1, 8] float32 (normalized)\n- `observation.language.tokens`: [1, 200] long (tokenized)\n- `observation.language.attention_mask`: [1, 200] long\n\nFor the postprocessor, I need to handle the action output from `predict_action_chunk` which gives me [1, chunk, 7] tensors, but the postprocessor pipeline expects a PolicyAction dict type, so there's a mismatch I need to resolve.\n\nLooking at the {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Hhat3WaqjL2RaaMQMAiH5z","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"PaliGemma is a gated model, so even with a mirror it needs authentication. Let me try a different approach: bypass the preprocessor pipeline entirely and do manual preprocessing.\n\nThe key steps for preprocessing:\n1. Load normalization stats from `policy_preprocessor_step_2_normalizer_processor.safetensors`\n2. Normalize state with mean/std\n3. Discretize state to 256 bins\n4. Create prompt string: `\"Task: {text}, State: {state_str};\\nAction: \"`\n\n\n5. eval_pi05_libero.py           # baseline (no pruning)\n    python eval_pi05_libero.py 0.5       # 50% random pruning\n\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, json, math, time as time_mod, logging, types\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import deque\n\nos.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = \"0\"\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, force=True, format=\"%(asctime)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# ─── Config ───\nCKPT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/pi05-libero-finetuned\"\nTOKENIZER_PATH = \"/root/.cache/huggingface/hub/models--unsloth--gemma-2b/snapshots/7ac9d201a57c8cdc6f939069fc0f044c60197a4a\"\nTASK_SUITE = \"libero_object\"\nN_EPISODES_PER_TASK = 50\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nPRUNE_RATIO = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.0\nSEED = 7\nREPLAN_STEPS = 5\nMAX_TOKEN_LEN = 200\nRESULTS_DIR = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_results\")\n\n# ─── Load model ───\nlog.info(f\"Loading Pi0.5 from {CKPT_DIR} ...\")\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import PI05Policy\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(CKPT_DIR)\npolicy = policy.to(DEVICE)\npolicy.eval()\n\nmodel = policy.model\ntotal_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\nlog.info(f\"Model loaded: {total_params/1e6:.1f}M params on {DEVICE}\")\n\n# ─── Load tokenizer ───\nlog.info(\"Loading tokenizer...\")\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(TOKENIZER_PATH)\ntokenizer.padding_side = \"right\"\n\n# ─── Load normalization stats ───\nnorm_stats = load_file(os.path.join(CKPT_DIR, \"policy_preprocessor_step_2_normalizer_processor.safetensors\"))\nstate_mean = norm_stats[\"observation.state.mean\"]  # [8]\nstate_std = norm_stats[\"observation.state.std\"]    # [8]\naction_mean = norm_stats[\"action.mean\"]            # [7]\naction_std = norm_stats[\"action.std\"]              # [7]\nNORM_EPS = 1e-8\n\n# ─── Vision token info ───\nN_PATCHES = (224 // 14) ** 2  # 256\nlog.info(f\"Vision tokens: {N_PATCHES} per image, 3 image slots -> {N_PATCHES*3} total\")\n\n# ─── Install pruning hook ───\nif PRUNE_RATIO > 0:\n    original_embed_prefix = model.embed_prefix\n\n    def pruned_embed_prefix(self_model, images, img_masks, tokens, masks):\n        embs = []\n        pad_masks_list = []\n        att_masks = []\n\n        for img, img_mask in zip(images, img_masks, strict=True):\n            img_emb = self_model.paligemma_with_expert.embed_image(img)\n            bsize, num_img_embs, dim = img_emb.shape\n\n            n_keep = max(1, int(num_img_embs * (1.0 - PRUNE_RATIO)))\n            indices = torch.randperm(num_img_embs, device=img_emb.device)[:n_keep].sort().values\n            img_emb = img_emb[:, indices, :]\n\n            embs.append(img_emb)\n            pad_masks_list.append(img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, n_keep))\n            att_masks += [0] * n_keep\n\n        lang_emb = self_model.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\n        lang_emb = lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb.shape[-1])\n        embs.append(lang_emb)\n        pad_masks_list.append(masks)\n        att_masks += [0] * lang_emb.shape[1]\n\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\n        pad_masks_out = torch.cat(pad_masks_list, dim=1)\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=pad_masks_out.device)\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(pad_masks_out.shape[0], -1)\n        return embs, pad_masks_out, att_masks\n\n    model.embed_prefix = types.MethodType(\n        lambda self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks: pruned_embed_prefix(self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks),\n        model\n    )\n    n_keep = max(1, int(N_PATCHES * (1 - PRUNE_RATIO)))\n    log.info(f\"Pruning: random {PRUNE_RATIO*100:.0f}% removal -> {n_keep}/{N_PATCHES} tokens/img\")\n\n# ─── LIBERO setup ───\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nnp.random.seed(SEED)\ntask_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[TASK_SUITE]()\nn_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\nmax_steps_map = {\"libero_object\": 280, \"libero_spatial\": 220, \"libero_goal\": 300, \"libero_10\": 520}\nmax_steps = max_steps_map.get(TASK_SUITE, 400)\n\nlog.info(f\"Suite: {TASK_SUITE}, {n_tasks} tasks x {N_EPISODES_PER_TASK} eps\")\n\n# ─── Helpers ───\ndef quat_to_axisangle(quat):\n    q = quat.copy()\n    q[3] = max(-1.0, min(1.0, q[3]))\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3]**2)\n    if abs(den) < 1e-8:\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * np.arccos(q[3]) / den).astype(np.float32)\n\ndef preprocess_obs(obs, task_text):\n    \"\"\"Convert LIBERO obs dict to model batch.\"\"\"\n    base_img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\n    wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]\n\n    # [C,H,W] float32 [0,1]\n    base_t = torch.from_numpy(base_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n    wrist_t = torch.from_numpy(wrist_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n\n    # State: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2) = 8\n    eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n    eef_quat = obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n    state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, quat_to_axisangle(eef_quat), gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\n    state_t = torch.from_numpy(state)\n\n    # Normalize state\n    norm_state = (state_t - state_mean) / (state_std + NORM_EPS)\n\n    # Discretize to 256 bins\n    state_np = norm_state.numpy()\n    state_np = np.clip(state_np, -1.0, 1.0)\n    bins = np.linspace(-1, 1, 257)[:-1]\n    discretized = np.digitize(state_np, bins) - 1\n    discretized = np.clip(discretized, 0, 255)\n\n    # Create prompt\n    cleaned_text = task_text.strip().replace(\"_\", \" \").replace(\"\\n\", \" \")\n    state_str = \" \".join(map(str, discretized))\n    prompt = f\"Task: {cleaned_text}, State: {state_str};\\nAction: \"\n\n    # Tokenize\n    enc = tokenizer(prompt, max_length=MAX_TOKEN_LEN, padding=\"max_length\",\n                    truncation=True, return_tensors=\"pt\")\n\n    # Build batch\n    batch = {\n        \"observation.images.image\": base_t.unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE),\n        \"observation.images.image2\": wrist_t.unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE),\n        \"observation.language.tokens\": enc.input_ids.to(DEVICE),\n        \"observation.language.attention_mask\": enc.attention_mask.to(DEVICE),\n    }\n    return batch\n\ndef unnormalize_actions(actions):\n    \"\"\"Unnormalize actions [B, chunk, 7] from normalized space.\"\"\"\n    a_mean = action_mean.to(actions.device)\n    a_std = action_std.to(actions.device)\n    return actions * a_std + a_mean\n\ndef get_libero_env(task, resolution, seed):\n    desc = task.language\n    bddl = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=str(bddl),\n                             camera_heights=resolution, camera_widths=resolution)\n    env.seed(seed)\n    return env, desc\n\n# ─── Eval loop ───\ntotal_success = 0\ntotal_episodes = 0\nresults = {\n    \"config\": {\n        \"model\": \"pi05\", \"ckpt\": CKPT_DIR, \"suite\": TASK_SUITE,\n        \"prune_ratio\": PRUNE_RATIO, \"n_eps_per_task\": N_EPISODES_PER_TASK,\n        \"seed\": SEED, \"vision_tokens_per_image\": N_PATCHES,\n        \"total_vision_tokens\": N_PATCHES * 3,\n        \"n_keep_per_image\": max(1, int(N_PATCHES * (1 - PRUNE_RATIO))) if PRUNE_RATIO > 0 else N_PATCHES,\n    },\n    \"per_task\": {},\n}\n\nt_start = time_mod.time()\nfor task_id in range(n_tasks):\n    task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n    init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n    env, task_desc = get_libero_env(task, 256, SEED)\n\n    task_successes = 0\n\n    for ep in range(N_EPISODES_PER_TASK):\n        env.reset()\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\n        for _ in range(10):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n\n        done = False\n        action_queue = deque()\n\n        for t in range(max_steps):\n            if not action_queue:\n                batch = preprocess_obs(obs, task_desc)\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    actions = policy.predict_action_chunk(batch)  # [1, chunk, action_dim]\n                actions = unnormalize_actions(actions)\n                action_np = actions[0].cpu().numpy()  # [chunk, 7]\n                for a in action_np[:REPLAN_STEPS]:\n                    action_queue.append(a[:7])\n\n            action = action_queue.popleft()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n            if done:\n                task_successes += 1\n                total_success += 1\n                break\n\n        total_episodes += 1\n\n        if (ep + 1) % 10 == 0:\n            log.info(f\"  T{task_id} [{task_desc[:40]}] ep={ep+1}: \"\n                     f\"{task_successes}/{ep+1}={task_successes/(ep+1)*100:.0f}%\")\n\n    task_sr = task_successes / N_EPISODES_PER_TASK\n    results[\"per_task\"][task_desc] = {\"id\": task_id, \"sr\": task_sr, \"n\": task_successes}\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - t_start\n    log.info(f\"Task {task_id}: SR={task_sr*100:.1f}% | \"\n             f\"Total: {total_success}/{total_episodes}={total_success/total_episodes*100:.1f}% | \"\n             f\"Time: {elapsed/60:.1f}min\")\n    env.close()\n\nresults[\"overall_sr\"] = total_success / total_episodes\nresults[\"total_successes\"] = total_success\nresults[\"total_episodes\"] = total_episodes\nresults[\"elapsed_min\"] = (time_mod.time() - t_start) / 60\n\nRESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\nif PRUNE_RATIO > 0:\n    fname = f\"pi05_random_prune_k{1-PRUNE_RATIO:.2f}_{TASK_SUITE}.json\"\nelse:\n    fname = f\"pi05_baseline_{TASK_SUITE}.json\"\nout_path = RESULTS_DIR / fname\nwith open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n    json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\nlog.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\nlog.info(f\"RESULT: enc.attention_mask.to(DEVICE),\n    }\n\n# LIBERO\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nnp.random.seed(7)\nsuite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[\"libero_object\"]()\ntask = suite.get_task(0)\ninit_states = suite.get_task_init_states(0)\nbddl = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\nenv = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=str(bddl), camera_heights=256, camera_widths=256)\nenv.seed(7)\ntask_desc = task.language\nlog.info(f\"Task: {task_desc}\")\n\nfor ep in range(2):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\n    for _ in range(10):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n\n    action_queue = deque()\n    done = False\n    for t in range(280):\n        if not action_queue:\n            batch = preprocess_obs(obs, task_desc)\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                actions = policy.predict_action_chunk(batch)\n            actions = actions * action_std.to(DEVICE) + action_mean.to(DEVICE)\n            action_np = actions[0].cpu().numpy()\n            for a in action_np[:5]:\n                action_queue.append(a[:7])\n\n        action = action_queue.popleft()\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        if done:\n            log.info(f\"  ep={ep} SUCCESS at step {t}\")\n            break\n\n    if not done:\n        log.info(f\"  ep={ep} FAIL (timeout)\")\n\nenv.close()\nlog.info(\"Smoke test complete!\")\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_017LhmBru8zWMUB1dgHikzNG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Found the issue! The `PaliGemmaModel.get_image_features()` returns `image_features` which is a tensor (after `multi_modal_projector`), not an object with `pooler_output`. But the `embed_image` method in the LeRobot Pi0.5 code expects `image_outputs.pooler_output`.\n\nThis means the LeRobot Pi0.5 code was written for a different version of transformers where `get_image_features` returns an object with `pooler_output`. Looking at the parent class implementation, it seems `get_image_features` extracts features from the vision tower but doesn't wrap them in the expected output structure, so I need to handle the tensor directly rather than trying to access a pooler_output property that doesn't exist.
